公司簡介 
亞德客成立於 1989 年，2009 年設立於開
曼群島的亞德客國際為控股主體 ， 是一家專
業生產各類氣動器材的集團 ， 公司主要從事
生產氣動控制元件 、 氣動執行原件 、 氣源處
理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造及銷售等業
務，以 「AirTAC」 自有品牌銷售於大中華地
區，中國市場佔營收 94％，台灣佔 2％、
海外佔 4％ ， 現為大陸市佔排名第二的氣動
元件廠，市佔率為30％，SMC 在中國市佔
率排名第一，達 33％。

主要競爭對手：SMC、Festo

林其美 iris.lin@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

亞德客-KY (1590 TT) 
保持成長趨勢

永豐觀點 
10 月公司日出貨量仍優於前幾個月，4Q26 營收表現仍不錯，預料將會與 3Q26
持平，AI 擴散效應 2027 年將持續發酵，建議逢低買進，區間操作。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進建議：理由包括：(1) 10 月公司日出貨量仍優於前幾個月，10 月應用領
域中仍有多個領域表現好 ， 這當中應有AI 擴散效應帶來電子業成長的貢獻 。4Q26
營收表現仍不錯，預料將會與 3Q26 持平，(2) 2026 全年維持先前營收成長高於
20%的看法，營業利益率則調高至 35%，氣動元件 2026、2027 年仍處於上升週
期，預估公司營收皆將保持雙位數成長，(3) 公司 2026 年 payout 比例 65%，預
計 2027 年將再提高。評價方面，公司本益比區間 20-40 倍，目前僅 16 倍，處於
相對低檔，AI 擴散效應持續發酵，2026、2027 年獲利在成長軌道上，建議逢低買
進，區間操作，目標價維持 1972 元（27X 2027EPS）。

ESG 評析 
亞德客企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 A 等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

2

亞德客為中國第二大氣動元件廠商：亞德客成立於 1989 年，2009 年設立於開曼
群島的亞德客國際為控股主體 ， 是一家專業生產各類氣動器材的集團， 公司主要從
事生產氣動控制元件、 氣動執行原件、 氣源處理元件及氣動輔助元件之製造及銷售
等業務，以「AirTAC」自有品牌銷售於大中華地區，中國市場佔營收 94％，台灣
佔 2％、海外佔4％，現為大陸市佔排名第二的氣動元件廠，市佔率為30％，SMC
在中國市佔率排名第一，達 33％。 
3Q26 EPS 達 14.23 元，符合預期：亞德客 3Q26 營收 115.12 億元 （+39.61%），
營業利益率 33.92%，稅後淨利 28.47 億元（+35.59%YoY），EPS 為 14.23 元，
符合預期。目前中國金屬原物料成本仍在高檔，以加第三季調漲薪資，不過在公司
降本增效的措施持續實施下，第三季營業利益率優於預期。3Q26 有效稅率拉升，
乃因公司有意增加 2027 年以後的 payout 比例 ， 從中國匯出獲利將加徵10%的稅
率。目前氣動元件稼動率高於 100%、線軌稼動率高於 40%，存貨水準 110 天相
對較低（130-140 天屬健康水準） 。 
2026 年全年營收將成長 20%以上，2027 年主要應用領域亦有雙位數成長：過去
氣動元件 2 年一個上升週期，2 年一個下降週期，本波 2025 年開始進入上升週
期，趨勢較溫和，預計成長可超過 2-3 年。2022-2024 年行業雙位數衰退，2025
低個位數成長、預估 2026 將高個位數成長，而 2027 年中位數成長。2026 年以
來公司每月出貨與訂單量皆優於預期 ， 與電子業AI 擴散效應 、 公司規格持續擴增 、
市占率提升等因素有關。行業應用別當中，電子業 3Q26 營收成長 33%，電子業
中 25%-30%與智慧手機有關，主要客人在產品上市前3-6 個月拉貨，預估客戶一
般規格新手機明年上半年上市，故公司 3Q26 手機應用領域成長 26%YoY，預估
可延續至第四季，今年與明年電子行業預估依舊可成長雙位數，此乃因為 AI 擴散
效應將延續；電池行業中國政府有調控政策，9 月公司應收下滑 10%YoY， 不過10
月又恢復 15%-20%的成長，故未來成長幅度仍需觀察，不過中國廠商移至國外擴
廠，對行業成長性仍是有助益；汽車行業公司市占率僅 10%-20%，在市占率持續
提升下，今年與明年雙位數成長；傳統行業如食品、包裝、工具機等受惠於政府補
貼政策，客戶積極擴充自動化產能，以及廠商回到中國擴廠，預估今年與明年依舊
雙位數成長；半導體受惠於中國國產化政策，過去每月營收 500 萬元人民幣，目
前已達 1300-1400 萬元，2027 年新規格上市，4Q26 可讓客戶開始認證，公司規
劃十年後年營收可達 10 億元人民幣。 
線軌市場市況仍在好轉中 ：線軌 2025 出貨量成長 25%，3Q26 營收成長 27%YoY，
今年市場傳動元件市場景氣回升 ， 螺桿大幅調漲價格 ， 線軌景氣也好轉 、 交期拉長 ，
但價格漲幅並不大。公司線軌營收也隨市場水漲船高，目前稼動率 40%（去年
20%+） ，預估年底時可達50%，屆時毛利率可達 30%，稼動率 80%時，毛利率
40%。公司規劃十年後年營收可達 30 億元人民幣。 
電控產品已開始貢獻營收：電控系統中國市場規模 1000 億元人民幣，但有很多不
同系統，公司會挑市場規模大的產品跨入，該產品資本支出低但毛利率高。過去幾
年利用五個系列產品在去年時營收達 4 億元人民幣，主要是搭配氣缸銷售傳感器，

營運現況與分析

### Page 3

3

今年將再推出 3 個系列，今年以來營收成長 20%以上。公司預估未來 10 年公司
有機會做到 30 億元人民幣年營收。 
10 月日出貨金額持續 MoM 成長、4Q26 預計營收可 QoQ 持平：10 月公司日出
貨量仍優於前幾個月，但因工作天數較少，營收不一定會高於 9 月，10 月應用領
域中仍有多個領域表現好 ， 這當中應有AI 擴散效應帶來電子業成長的貢獻 。4Q26
營收表現仍不錯，預料將會與3Q26 持平，2026 全年維持先前營收成長高於 20%
的看法，營業利益率則調高至 35%（先前為 34%） 。至於2027 年，前述氣動元件
主要應用領域皆呈現雙位數的成長，線軌成長預計可超過 20%，在線軌稼動率提
升、原物料平穩下，預計營業利益仍可維持高檔。 
財務預估：預估 2026 年合併營收為 451.46 億元(+31.5％YoY)，稅後淨利 118.13
億元(+40.6％YoY)，EPS 為 59.07 元 。 預估2027 年合併營收為 533.99 億元(+18.28
％YoY)，稅後淨利 143.44 億元(+21.42％YoY)，EPS 為 71.72 元。

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國 9 月 ISM 非製造業 PMI 從 8 月的 55.4 降至 54.9，顯示服務業活動增速放
緩，然而國內需求強勁使供應鏈承壓，並推動投入價格指數由 72.6 升至 74.0 為
四年多來新高；另一方面，美國 10 月密西根大學消費者信心指數由 9 月的 48.1
降至 46.3，為連續第三個月下降且創 5 月以來最低 ， 惟未來一年通膨預期由 4.6%
升至 4.7%，顯示通膨可能持續維持高位至 2027 年。
⚫ 聯準會 9 月 FOMC 會議紀要顯示與會官員均支持升息，並認為年底前尚需升息
一次 ，但對於後續升息時點及升息理由上存在分歧； 其中部分官員認為升息是防
止能源及其他價格衝擊造成更廣泛影響 的預防性措施，但更為鷹派官員則認為
升息在於抑制投資和支出，以防範正在顯現的需求驅動型通膨。
⚫ 歐洲央行 9 月貨幣政策會議紀要顯示全體決策委員均同意升息，並認為當前經
濟成長風險偏向下行 ， 通膨風險則偏向上行 ，因此將保留政策彈性， 根據經濟數

據做為決策，不提供未來利率路徑指引。
⚫ 台灣 9 月 CPI 年增率自 8 月 2.05%上升至 2.73%，已連續 5 個月超過 2%通膨警
戒線 ， 且創兩年半來最大漲幅 ，除了豪雨導致蔬果價格上漲及中秋假期的短期因
素外，油價居高不下亦使通膨壓力增加。
⚫ 中國央行發布匯率政策立場聲明， 強調中國貿易發展根源於產業國際競爭力的
提升，無必要通過匯率貶值獲取貿易競爭優勢 ，並指出中國長期實施以市場供需
為基礎、參考一籃子貨幣進行調節管理浮動匯率制度。

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/12/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.699，低點在 31.940，以 31.875 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 4.5 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶，週初，受到前週公布的美國非農就業數據低於預期，降低
市場對聯準會 10 月升息的預期， 台股創高吸引外資匯入 ， 推升台幣最高來到31.699；
不過隨著美伊緊張情勢升溫且台灣連假將至 ， 台股收低使外資轉為大幅賣超並匯出 ，
推低台幣回到 9 月中以來的震盪區間下緣，最低觸及 31.940，最終在央行調節之下
收在 31.875。 
＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國 10 月密西根大學消費者信心
指數持續下滑，由 9 月的 48.1 降至 46.3，創 5 月以來最低，惟未來一年通膨預期
升至 4.7%，加上中東局勢反覆，油價高位震盪，仍支撐市場對於聯準會年內升息
的預期，預計美元指數維持在 102 附近震盪，對台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，
台灣 9 月出口創歷史新高，年增 60.9%，反映 AI 及科技需求持續強勁，出口商後
續拋匯需求可望提供台幣支撐；然而，台股逼近五萬點關卡之際，外資未能延續買
超走勢，且連假前已大幅賣超台股，若後續外資持續匯出，將限制台幣升值空間。
綜合以上，預估本週台幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.700-32.000

走勢 ， 強調日本已脫離通縮 ， 將完全尊重央行政策獨立性 ， 暗示不反對進一步升息 。
面對通膨加劇與日圓走貶壓力，其過往堅持的貨幣寬鬆立場已出現轉向。然而目前
美日實質利差仍大，持續為 USD/JPY 提供支撐。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，
USD/JPY 持續沿著上彎的月線上攻，然匯價持續承壓於季線下方，在有效站上季線
及突破前高壓力前，仍以區間震盪看待。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震
盪的可能性較高。

壓。二、法國社會動盪與支出訴求阻礙財政減赤進程，加劇債務可持續性疑慮，並
推升法德公債利差與信用風險，令歐元承壓走弱。相較美國經濟展現韌性且聯準會
維持偏鷹立場，歐洲深陷景氣放緩與財政掣肘，歐洲央行緊縮空間受限。在歐美基
本面與政策前景持續分歧下 ，EUR/USD 缺乏實質支撐 ， 後市將維持偏弱整理並下探
關鍵支撐。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價雖多次向下測試 1.1165 關
鍵價位均獲得支撐，惟每次反彈均無法站上五日線，且各天期均線下彎呈現空頭排
列。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現區間偏空震盪的可能性較高。

Document 077: 2026金融街论坛年会解读解读：筑牢流动性防线，提升制度包容性

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026金融街论坛年会解读解读：筑牢流动性防线，提升制度包容性.pdf
Report source: 国新证券股份
Date: 2026-11-01
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

市场研究部 2026年10月20日
事件
10 月 19 日，2026 年金融街论坛年会召开，央行、金融监管
总局、证监会、国家外汇管理局主要负责人分别发表演讲。
解读
一、央行行长潘功胜的主题演讲的主要政策
一是将会同金融监管总局、中国证监会设立应对非银机构大额
赎回的流动性支持工具。
二是扩大常备借贷便利（SLF）的适用机构范围。
三是推动银行间和交易所债券市场互联互通落地。
四是试点进一步便利境外金融机构在境内外汇市场开展人民币
购售业务。
五是会同科技部等 10 个部门出台《关于金融支持建设北京
（京津冀）、上海（长三角）、粤港澳大湾区国际科技创新中心的
指导意见》。
此外，今年 6月，人民银行设立了境外央行回购工具，支持境
外央行或货币当局、国际金融组织、主权财富基金，通过债券回购
方式从人民银行获取人民币流动性，便利其人民币流动性管理和人
民币资产配置。
今年初，人民银行平稳切换了数字人民币新计量框架，数字人
民币定位由现金切换为存款货币。
二、金融监管总局局长丁向群主题演讲的主要政策

切实提升监管有效性，有力防控金融风险。一是坚持风险导向
强化金融监管。二是适应实践需要健全金融法治。三是突出重点环
节提高监管能力。
加力推动改革转型，强化高质量发展金融支撑。一是不断优化
机构布局。二是大力规范市场秩序。三是持续提升金融服务适配性。
始终坚持互利共赢，持续推动金融高水平开放。一是积极拓展
金融开放新空间。二是有效服务国际经贸往来。三是推动完善全球
金融治理体系。
宏观研究
筑牢流动性防线，提升制度包容性
——2026金融街论坛年会解读解读
市场表现截至 2026.10.20
资料来源：国家统计局,国新证券整理。
分析师：郝大明
登记编码：S1490514010002
邮箱：haodaming@crsec.com.cn
证券研究报告

### Page 2

三、证监会主席吴清主题演讲的主要政策
第一，坚持稳字当头，全力维护资本市场安全稳定。
第二，突出守正创新，持续深化资本市场包容性改革。
第三，强化依法从严，大力培育规范健康的市场生态。
第四，深化务实合作，有序扩大市场高水平对外开放。
四、外管局局长朱鹤新主题演讲的主要政策
近期，在存量政策提质增效基础上，结合新形势新要求，以及
调研中发现的企业新的实际需求，拟集中推出一批增量措施，支持
企业更好参与国际合作与竞争，更好利用两个市场、两种资源。
一是推出经常项目便利化一揽子政策，更好满足外贸企业多样
化结算需求，支持贸易新业态新模式发展，促进外贸提质增效。
二是推出优化资本项目外汇管理一揽子政策，更好满足企业跨
境投融资和资金配置需求，服务“引进来”和“走出去”，拓展双
向投资合作空间。
三是推动外汇市场开放发展，扩大证券公司结售汇业务试点，
支持金融机构做好企业特别是中小企业汇率风险管理服务，提升跨
境服务能力，更好支持离在岸金融市场协同发展，助力人民币国际
化。
五、我们的解读
1、筑牢流动性防线，巩固完善稳市机制建设

维护市场稳定是资本市场健康发展的前提。人民银行行长潘功
胜宣布，将会同金融监管总局、证监会设立应对非银机构大额赎回
流动性支持工具，专门针对资管机构遭遇集中赎回、债券市场陷入
“赎回—抛售”负向循环的风险场景，通过高等级抵押品融资，切
断风险自我放大链条，补齐非银体系流动性应急保障短板，提升金
融体系抗冲击能力。这一安排与此前偏重银行体系的流动性管理形
成互补，将非银机构纳入宏观审慎管理视野，显著增强了金融体系
应对突发冲击的韧性。同时，央行也将同步推出扩大常备借贷便利
适用机构范围、推动交易所与银行间债券市场互联互通落地等配套
举措，打通跨市场资金流转渠道，优化货币市场利率调控框架，为
资本市场营造稳定适宜的货币环境。
证监会主席吴清释放了两个具有即时稳市场的信号。一是同意
首批 18只主动 ETF注册，明确将其定位为“推动基金产品创新发
展的重要举措，为市场引入更多增量资金”。在宽基 ETF 已成为
近期市场企稳重要力量的背景下，主动 ETF 的扩容意味着增量资
金入市渠道的进一步拓宽。二是强调“高度警惕境外挂钩 A股个股

的杠杆产品等新风险点”，强化外部风险输入的前置防范，体现了
监管层在开放环境下对新型风险的前瞻性关注。另外，在中长期稳
市机制方面，吴清主席明确提出要“推动进一步健全适配长期投资

### Page 3

的长周期考核机制，扩大中长期资金来源、渠道和入市方式，持续
深化公募基金改革，壮大市场稳定力量”。这意味着政策思路正从
短期的流动性干预，转向培育具有逆周期属性的制度性资本。当公
募基金、保险资金、养老金等真正以年度甚至跨年度维度评估投资
绩效时，市场的非理性波动才有望获得结构性的平抑力量。
2、强化银行保险业风险治理，夯实中长期资金根基
金融监管总局局长丁向群从机构端风险防控、行业改革、对外
开放角度，明确与资本市场改革相配套的监管导向。丁向群强调要
打好风险化解主动仗，稳妥处置重点领域风险，健全风险监测预警
与早期纠正机制，实现全部金融活动监管 “全覆盖、无例外”，
消除跨市场、跨业态监管盲区，守住不发生系统性风险底线。监管
持续强化银行保险机构的风险约束，引导保险资金等长期资金规范
参与资本市场投资，不能理解为中长期资金的收缩，而是旨在通过
净化机构生态，为资本市场投融资功能发挥筑牢中介资金基础，防
范机构风险向股票、债券市场传导溢出。另外，丁向群表示将支持
更多外资机构和长期资本来华展业兴业，鼓励外资机构发挥财富管

理、养老金融领域专业优势，有助于扩大中长期资金的来源，进一
步巩固资本市场发展的稳定性。
3、深化资本市场包容性改革，为新质生产力铺设制度通道
以资本市场服务科技创新，是本次论坛改革主线。吴清主席强
调未来将“落实落细‘两创板’改革举措”、“实施更具包容性适
应性的发行上市、并购重组等制度”、“择机发布新修订的上市公
司证券发行注册管理办法”，三项表述层层递进，核心是让那些处
于成长期、尚未盈利但具备核心技术能力的企业，能够更顺畅地获
得直接融资支持。这一监管信号与当前市场结构的变化形成了有意
味的呼应。当前科技板块尽管经历了剧烈调整，但其盈利预期并未
被系统性下调，电子行业近四周上调 2027年业绩预测的公司占比
仍居前列。调整消化的主要是前期过高的估值溢价与拥挤的交易结
构，而非产业逻辑本身。此时本次强调未来将出台更具包容性的制
度安排，实质上是在市场情绪的低位区间，为下一轮科技企业的资
本化提前铺设通道。同时，吴清主席提出“加快推动出台上市公司
监管条例”与“加力推进证券公司高质量发展行动”，则从公司治

理与中介机构两个层面，为包容性改革提供了质量约束与专业支撑，
避免包容性演变为降低标准的代名词。
4、强化投资端保护，修复市场信心锚点
信心的修复，离不开对中小投资者合法权益的实质性保障。吴
清主席在本次论坛上明确推出的四项近期举措，直指市场关切的痛
点。其中，完善违规减持处罚规则与制定发布证券市场先行赔付指
导意见尤为值得关注。前者通过细化认定标准、坚持过罚相当，遏
制利用信息优势与持股成本优势进行监管套利的行为，维护了二级
市场交易的公平性；后者则通过明确适用主体与实施程序，为受损

### Page 4

投资者提供了更为便捷高效的救济渠道。从市场反馈看，10 月 19
日上证指数收涨 0.39%，银行等低估值板块表现稳健，一定程度上
反映了政策信号对短期情绪的稳定作用。尽管单日涨幅不宜过度解
读，但监管层将“保护投资者”从原则宣示转化为可操作的制度安
排，其累积效应将逐步体现在市场风险溢价的回落之中。
5、统筹开放与安全，拓展资本市场境内外配置价值
在外部环境复杂性上升的背景下，资本市场的高水平开放并未
止步，而是呈现出更强调“可预期性”与“风险可控”的特征。国
家外汇管理局局长朱鹤新公布一批外汇领域增量改革措施，其中优
化资本项目外汇管理一揽子政策和扩大证券公司结售汇业务试点等
举措，能直接服务跨境投融资便利化，便利境外机构投资 A股，也
便利境内主体参与全球市场配置。吴清主席也提出提升 QFII 投资
运作便利度、支持境内外机构合作推出基于中国资产的指数产品，
并加快推进人民币股票交易柜台与 REITs纳入沪深港通。这些举措
的深层逻辑在于，通过降低跨境交易的制度性摩擦成本，吸引更多

以配置为导向的长期外资流入。
当前沪深 300 滚动市盈率处于历史中低位区间，全 A 市盈率
中位数较年中高点已有明显回落，中国资产在全球范围内的相对性
价比正在改善。开放举措的稳步推进，为全球投资者分享中国经济
结构转型的红利提供了更畅通的渠道，同时也在资本项下形成“有
管理的流入”格局，避免短期投机资本大进大出对市场稳定性造成
冲击。
综上，本次金融街论坛出台的政策组合，反映出监管对于未来
资本市场稳健发展的完整逻辑：以流动性支持工具守住不发生系统
性风险的底线，以包容性制度改革培育增量优质供给，以投资者保
护举措修复市场信任，以有序开放引导长期资本配置。相关政策并
非孤立的举措，而是多部门协同的系统安排。短期重在缓释市场冲
击、稳定市场预期，中长期重在完善资本市场基础制度，理顺投资
与融资的平衡关系。预计随着政策效力的逐步释放，资本市场投资
与融资相协调的功能将更为健全，其在服务“十五五”经济社会高
质量发展中的枢纽作用，也将获得更为坚实的制度支撑。
投资建议
10月 19日召开的金融街论坛年会上，央行、金融监管总局、

证监会、国家外汇管理局主要负责人集中发声，围绕流动性保障、
制度创新、风险防控、高水平开放打出政策“组合拳”，从货币环
境、机构治理、投融资改革、跨境资本管理等多个维度，为资本市
场稳预期、强韧性、促改革搭建完整政策框架，有效回应了市场关
切，为下一阶段资本市场稳健发展提供了清晰的政策支撑。
风险提示
1、地缘政治恶化超预期；2、欧美经济衰退超预期。

Realtek (2379.TW)

6
Realtek
Company description
Founded in 1987, Realtek is a leading fabless semiconductor with
headquarters in Hsinchu, Taiwan. It has an extensive global footprint with
operational offices in China, Japan, Korea, Singapore, and the US. Realtek's
product portfolio is well-diversified; generally, it can be categorized into three
business groups: Communications Network (Wi-Fi, Bluetooth, automotive
Ethernet, PC Ethernet, switch, etc.), Computer Peripherals (audio codec,

clock IC, card reader, IP cam SoC, USB  hub controller, Type-C controller, SSD
controller) and Multimedia (TV SoC, LCD controller, STB SoC, etc.).
Investment strategy
We rate the shares Buy. We believe Realtek is well-positioned to ride on
continued specs upgrade demand for WiFi, switch and 10G PON as well as the
strong advancements in the automotive and Io T sectors. In the long term, we
believe Realtek's cost-effective, high-performance, and comprehensive wired

and wireless solutions should become more critical with the growing
connectivity requirement at the edge.
Valuation
Our target price for Realtek is set at NT$900 based on our target PE multiple
of 20x to our 2027 EPS. We believe our PER of 20x, higher than its 3-year
forward PER average, is justified by its solid growth opportunity in automotive
and switch/PON business. We also believe its continued efforts in products
mix optimization should help to support its margins expansion. At our target

price, the shares would trade at a 2026/27E P/B of 8x/6x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) Slowdown in technology
migration for its networking business; 2) Weaker-than-expected growth
momentum for automotive Ethernet sales; 3) Worse-than-expected PC/NB
and consumer electronic demand; 4) Slower-than-expected GM expansion
due to pricing competition from peers; and 5) Higher-than-expected memory
cost burden to dampen the order outlook from its networking and PC/NB

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

股價相對大盤走勢

欣銓 (3264 TT)  Buy

晶圓大廠新訂單逐步向上，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：欣銓 2026 年資本支出轉向積極擴張，主要來自與策
略客戶 (晶圓代工廠 )已確定之 ASIC 合作案，其龍潭新廠無塵室
與基礎設施建置，以及子公司全智科配合美系客戶擴產。 1Q26
至 3Q26 期間取得不動產、廠房及設備 (資本支出 )累計達 102.74
億元，相較於 3Q25 累計之 27.96 億元大幅增加 74.78 億元
(YoY+267.45%) 。3Q26 已進入量產，下一階段擴產將自 2Q27 啟
動，規劃自 2Q27 起每季開出一層全新無塵室加入量產，後續樓
層無塵室擴建將以現有主要客戶需求為優先配合對象。預估欣
銓 2027 年稅後 EPS 13.20 元。龍潭廠新專案是 2026~2027 年最
重要的增長引擎。而旗下全智湖口新廠，也於 3Q26 正式進入矽
光子相關新產品的量產階段，都將帶動公司中長期營收獲利向
上，因此建議欣銓 Buy ，目標價 322 元。  
3Q26 稅後 EPS 3.01 元：龍潭新廠歷經 1H26 客戶驗證與進場裝
機後，於 07/2026 起正式進入量產階段，帶動欣銓營收成長，
3Q26 營收為 53.37 億元， QoQ+17.72% 。營收依應用別分析，通
訊 Communication/Connectivity 佔 41.0% ；車用 / 安控
(ATV/Security) 佔 17.9% ；射頻晶片 (RF IC) 佔 10.9% ；微處理器
(General MCU) 佔 8.7%；Storage 佔 9.5%；PC/Consumers 佔 6.6%；
Memory 佔 3.7% ；其他 1.8% 。毛利率的部份， 3Q26 毛利率
45.43% ，增加 5.27 個百分點。 3Q26 稅後 EPS 3.01 元。  
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 3.03 元：受到 3Q26 營收基期墊高、歐美
客戶於 11/2026 至 12/2026 進入假期拉貨淡季 ， 以及手機與通訊
終端產品季節性調節，營收僅小幅成長，毛利率展望方面，由
於 4Q26 未有新無塵室開出，且公司需提前為 2027 年擴產招募
人力、提列初期整備成本與設備折舊增加，預期 4Q26 毛利率將
略低於 3Q26 之 45.43% ，但仍可穩健維持高檔水準；公司努力
進行成本管控和改善產品組合 ， 讓 4Q26 獲利有機會高於 3Q26。
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 3.03 元。 2026 年稅後 EPS 10.28 元。

2 群益投顧 
  欣銓 ESG ：  
欣銓積極推動  ESG( 環境、社會、公司治理 )相關政策與行動，並於官方網站
設有專屬  ESG 頁面與歷年永續報告書，公開揭露永續發展成果與目標。  
環境 (Environmental) 
•持續推動再生能源與減碳：延續加入  RE100 的承諾，積極推動節能減碳
與再生能源採購，並簽署 SBTi( 科學基礎減碳目標倡議 )承諾，強化減碳目標
與國際接軌。  
•循環經濟新提案：攜手供應鏈夥伴推動循環箱再進化，落實資源再利用與
減廢。  
•生物多樣性與生態尊重：持續舉辦淨灘、認養友善田等活動，強調尊重生
態、友善健康。  
社會 (Social) 
•同仁參與與文化深化：舉辦「 ESG Award 」專案競賽，鼓勵員工提出永續點
子與專案，涵蓋綠色製造、責任供應鏈、多元包容職場、人才培育、關懷弱
勢等五大方向。  
• 員工關懷與職場環境：持續改善員工宿舍環境，提升同仁生活品質。  
•社會公益：贊助在地傳統藝文活動、捐助弱勢團體，並與產業園區合作舉
辦移工籃球賽，促進多元共融。  
公司治理 (Governance) 
•資訊透明與永續報告：發行永續報告書，持續自主揭露非財務資訊，強調
資訊透明與溝通成效。  
•國際認證與標準：完成 RBA VAP 8.0 改版訓練，強化人權、環保等國際標
準合規與超越法規要求。  
•永續獎項肯定：榮獲「英國標準協會 (BSI)ESG 永續發展領航獎」及「台灣
企業永續獎 (TCSA) －永續報告獎金獎」 ，肯定公司在永續發展、資訊揭露與
社會責任的表現。  
攜手合作夥伴擴大永續影響力，推動以大帶小、標竿學習。展現穩健落實永
續目標與全球標準接軌的實力。  
◼CDP 氣候問卷榮獲  A-List 最高肯定 ： 公司在2025 年  CDP 全球環境評
比中，從全球逾  22,100 家企業脫穎而出，「氣候變遷」問卷榮獲 A 級領
導等級，「水安全」蟬聯  A- 領導等級肯定。  
◼ SBTi 近程目標通過驗證：公司設定的近程減碳目標已於 04/2026 正式通
過科學基礎減量目標倡議 (SBTi) 官方驗證。

◼龍潭廠榮獲「 2026 台灣建築永續獎金獎」肯定。

3 群益投顧 
由於全球  AI 需求極其暢旺，許多一線封測同業將產能高度集中在  AI 核
心晶片，導致其他「 AI 周邊晶片」或「非  AI 通訊晶片」的測試產能出現
缺口。欣銓因此獲得大量的外溢訂單 (轉單 )，特別是在通訊與 PC Consumer 
領域。欣銓 3Q26 營收為 53.37 億元， QoQ+17.72% 。  
營收依應用別分析，通訊 Communication/Connectivity 佔 41.0% ；車用 /安控
(ATV/Security) 佔 17.9%； 射頻晶片(RF IC) 佔 10.9%； 微處理器(General MCU)
佔 8.7% ； Storage 佔 9.5% ； PC/Consumers 佔 6.6% ； Memory 佔 3.7% ；其他
1.8% 。  
3Q26 終端應用結構，通訊與連網 (Communication/Connectivity) 為最大成長
引擎，營收佔比自 2Q26 的 33.4% 大幅擴張至 41.0% 。這部份含光通訊和晶
圓代工廠的訂單 。 車用與安控(ATV/Security) 營收佔比為 17.9%，略 低 於2Q26
的 18.2% ，不過營收金額是增加，車用市場回溫，營收持續向上。射頻晶片
(RF IC) 營收佔比為 10.9% ，低於 2Q26 的 12.2% ，因為手機拉貨動能下滑。
儲存裝置 (Storage) 營收佔比自 2Q26 的 10.8% 收斂至 9.5% ，因晶圓代工廠的
產能受限。通用型微控制器 (General MCU) 佔比為 8.7% ，相較 2Q26 之 8.5%
微幅增加。個人電腦與消費性晶片 (PC/Consumers) 佔比為 6.6% ，相較 2Q26
之 9.9% 明顯下降，需求受到終端價格上漲影響，加上客戶產品組合上也會
優先供應通訊產品，營收佔比下滑較明顯。記憶體 (Memory) 營收佔比為
3.7% ，低於 2Q26 的 5.4% 。其他類別佔比則由 1.60% 微升至 1.80% 。  
毛利率的部份，得益於通訊連網晶片拉貨動能強勁以及整體稼動率維持高
檔約 80%。營業毛利達 24.25 億元， QoQ+33.1% ， YoY+89.1% ，毛利率攀升
至 45.43% ，較 2Q26 增加 5.27 個百分點，高稼動率帶動規模經濟發酵。營
業利益達 18.6 億元， QoQ+35.0% ，營益率達 34.8% ，較 2Q26 成長 4.4 個百
分點，較 3Q25 大幅增加 10.5 個百分點。業外收支部分， 3Q26 業外淨支出
為 0.34 億元，稅前淨利 18.25 億元， QoQ+25.16% ，稅後 EPS 3.01 元。創下
單季獲利新高紀錄。  
累計前三季 (1Q26 至 3Q26) 合併營收達 138.54 億元， YoY+35.4% ，毛利率達
41.7% ，營業利益達 43.34 億元， YoY+69.60% ，營益率達 31.3% ，累計稅後
淨利為 35.54 億元， YoY+78.4% ，前三季累計 EPS 達 7.49 元。  
就客戶類型分析 ，1Q26 至 3Q26 累計營收中 ， 無晶圓廠IC 設計業者 (Fabless)
貢獻 52.5% ，垂直整合製造商 (IDM) 佔 42.1% ，晶圓代工廠 (Foundry) 佔比由
前三季累計的 1.8% 提高至 5.4%，其他客戶佔比為 0.1%。在產品測試型態方
面，前三季晶圓測試 (Wafer Sorting) 營收佔比達 73.2% ，成品測試 (Final Test)
佔比為 26.1% ，其他項目佔 0.7% 。  
基於與策略客戶已談定的  ASIC 合作案，預估欣銓 2026 年資本支出將明
顯增加至約 130 億元， 2027 年將會高於 2026 年：  
欣銓對資本支出一向保持保守與謹慎，主因是高額折舊若遇上稼動率下滑，
會嚴重衝擊毛利率。此次  2026 年轉向積極擴張，主要來自與策略客戶已確
定的  ASIC 合作案。欣銓 2024 至 2025 年的資本支出主要集中在龍潭新廠
的廠房興建與土建尾款； 2026 年則轉向以「機台設備」為主，廠房支出比
例將顯著降低。先進測試機台交期長達 6 - 8 個月。目前測試機台約 1,700
台以上。  
欣銓在 1Q26 至 3Q26 期間取得不動產 、 廠房及設備(資本支出 )累計達 102.74

4 群益投顧 
億元，相較於 3Q25 累計之 27.96 億元大幅增加 74.78 億元， YoY+267.45% ，
資本支出顯著放量主要由兩大核心動能驅動：第一為龍潭新廠無塵室與基
礎設施建置，第二為子公司全智科技為配合美系通訊大客戶強勁 AI 晶片測
試需求而積極採購高階測試設備。 4Q26 因實際投入量產的機台數量相對有
限，單季資本支出將較 3Q26 持平或微幅縮減，預估 2026 年資本支出約 130
億元。但 2027 年資本支出規模掌握度高且明確將超越 2026 年。主因龍潭新
廠單一樓層無塵室與機台設備支出即達 40 億元以上，公司規劃自 2Q27 起
每季開出 1 層全新無塵室加入量產， 2027 年約有 3 個樓層陸續投產，且後
續樓層的無塵室擴建將以現有主要客戶需求為優先配合對象。  
截至 3Q26 底，公司不動產、廠房及設備帳面價值擴張至 308.94 億元，佔總
資產比重達 64% ，資產總計同步推升至 485.56 億元。  
為支應擴產資金需求，公司除於 1Q26 至 3Q26 透過銀行借款淨增 61.43 億
元外，子公司於 09/2026 處分欣銓股票取得約 40 億元流動資金，後續將以
股利形式匯回母公司以支應資本支出。為因應 2027 年更為龐大的資本支出
銓現階段仍以銀行借款為優先籌資工具，預期負債比率將自 3Q26 底的 50%
進一步拉升，但公司承諾將整體負債比率嚴格控制在 60% 以內，並視擴產階
段適時評估其他籌資工具；同時因擴建資金留存考量，現金股利配發率預計
將由 2026 年的 70% 常態區間下修至 2027 年的 50% 以上。  
欣銓在海外生產基地的布局策略上，維持專注鞏固台灣、新加坡及中國南京
等現有廠區的營運效率與產能發揮。管理層明確指出，赴新國家或地區設立
生產基地必須具備足夠的在地客戶基礎與充沛的訂單量支撐，才能避免新
廠陷入長期營運虧損；基於當前尚未取得足額訂單承諾的考量，欣銓目前並
無赴美國投資建廠的計畫。  
在現有海外據點的產品與客戶定位方面，高階 AI ASIC 與先進製程相關的
測試訂單全數集中於台灣本地廠區，包括龍潭廠及全智科湖口廠。新加坡廠
則以承接成熟產品線為主，產品結構涵蓋車用、工控及消費性晶片，主要原
因在於新加坡當地目前尚無大量先進晶圓代工產能；後續欣銓在新加坡廠
區的成長策略，將聚焦於進一步深化與聯電 (UMC) 及世界先進 (VIS) 在新加
坡當地晶圓廠的委外測試合作機會。  
折舊費用方面，欣銓已公告自 1/1/2025 起，部分機器設備耐用年限由 7 年
延長至 9 年，這將有助於反映資產實際經濟效益，欣銓 2025 年折舊費用
31.39 億元。龍潭廠量產機台之折舊費用已有部分於 3Q26 開始提列，但因
部分設備驗收時點落於季中之後，折舊並未全額反映在 3Q26 單季，預期
4Q26 折舊費用將進一步高於 3Q26 ，預估 2026 年折舊費用約 35 億元。進入
2027 年後，新設備折舊將隨各季機台進場與驗收進度按部就班提列；依產
業慣例，新機台通常需於投產前一季進場進行裝機與驗證，例如 2Q27 投產
之產能將於 1Q27 進場裝機，驗收完成後即開始提列折舊。儘管折舊費用持
續墊高且 4Q26 至 1Q27 提前招募人力與產能整備將使毛利率自 3Q26 的
45.43% 高點略為回落，但因龍潭廠 4Q26 產能利用率維持滿載、全智湖口新
廠產能將於 2027 年達全產全銷，產能高稼動率預期可有效稀釋固定折舊成
本，使單季毛利率持續穩健維持在 40% 以上。  
產能利用率已達滿載，預估欣銓 4Q26 營收 55.24 億元， QoQ+3.50% ，稅前
淨利 18.57 億元， QoQ+1.75% ，稅後 EPS 3.03 元：  
展望 4Q26 營運，欣銓預估合併營收將呈現 QoQ+3% 左右，主要受到 3Q26

5 群益投顧 
營收基期墊高、歐美客戶於 11/2026 至 12/2026 進入假期拉貨淡季，以及手
機與通訊終端產品季節性調節所致。  
旗下全智總坪數逾 3,500 坪的湖口新廠，也於 3Q26 正式進入矽光子相關新
產品的量產階段，預期 4Q26 可以開出 20-25% 的產能，全智湖口廠客戶需
求高，因此 2027 年可以達到滿載狀況。  
毛利率展望方面，由於 4Q26 未有新無塵室開出，且公司需提前為 2027 年
擴產招募人力、提列初期整備成本與設備折舊增加，預期 4Q26 毛利率將略
低於 3Q26 之 45.43% ，但仍可穩健維持在高檔水準。定價策略方面，欣銓對
主力客戶價格維持穩定，但已針對低毛利與零星客戶提出調價，部分客戶已
順利反映漲價。公司努力進行成本管控和改善產品組合，讓 4Q26 獲利有機
會高於 3Q26。預估欣銓 4Q26 營收 55.24 億元， QoQ+3.50% ，稅前淨利 18.57
億元， QoQ+1.75% ，稅後 EPS 3.03 元。  
預估欣銓 2026 年營收 193.78 億元， YoY+38.13% ，稅前淨利 63.05 億元，
YoY+74.58% ，稅後 EPS 10.28 元。  
欣銓提供完整的光電測試 (全智 )與先進異質整合封裝 (瑞峰 )解決方案，建構
完整 CPO 生態系：  
欣銓集團在矽光子領域的布局完整，透過結合母公司與旗下轉投資公司的
資源，建構完整 CPO( 共封裝光學 )生態系，欣銓與全智皆為  SEMI 矽光子
產業聯盟及異質整合矽光子產業聯盟的重要成員。欣銓已布局光通訊與矽
光子 (CPO) 高階測試架構，目前接洽並配合之客戶達三家以上，測試製程至
少已切入至 Insertion 1 和 3 階段，並持續往 Insertion 4 進行技術評估與了
解。由於目前全球矽光子晶片出貨量仍處於技術開發與初期工程驗證階段，
市場尚未進入大規模營收爆發期，因此公司尚未設定具體之 CPO 營收貢獻
目標，但已透過全智湖口廠與美系通訊核心客戶建立緊密夥伴關係，提早卡
位高速傳輸光電整合封裝之長期測試商機。  
欣銓集團的矽光子佈局中，全智科技 (欣銓 100% 轉投資 )主要負責  EIC 測
試、PIC 測試以及光電整合測試 。 其中 EIC 測試已進入量產多年 ， 並於 2024 
年建置矽光子專用的光電整合測試實驗室，提供全方位的工程測試與驗證
服務。瑞峰半導體 (欣銓投資約  25%) 專注於先進異質整合封裝 (如 CPO 3D 
TSV/CoW ， EIC+PIC) 。瑞峰負責將 EIC 晶圓疊加在 PIC 晶圓上 (Chip on 
Wafer) ，隨後再交由全智進行光電整合測試。 Fabrinet( 策略投資夥伴 )主要負
責後續的  CPO 相關製程與光學引擎等項目。  
全智 (湖口新廠 )3Q26 正式量產，總面積超過  3,500 坪的湖口新廠，將著重
於矽光子測試。根據客戶端訊息，該廠區矽光子新產品的產能利用率拉升速
度預期將會相當快，且已與客戶洽談，有機會在  2027 年接近滿載。在  CPO 
的  EIC/PIC 測試產能上，目前已佈建超過  150 台設備。中長期擴廠計畫，
為因應強勁的光通訊客戶需求，湖口廠目前正在整理無塵室空間，希望能再
騰出 1~2 層樓來配合擴充；同時，為 2028 年以後的長期成長，公司也已準
備著手尋求購買新土地。  
瑞峰已於  2024 年建置第一條  12 吋矽光子晶圓級封裝 (WLP) 生產線。目
前將  EIC 疊在  PIC 晶圓上的  Chip on Wafer 製程正逐漸進入量產。 2025 
年已導入數家新客戶，進行超過  30 項  NPI( 新產品導入 )驗證； 2026 年更

6 群益投顧 
預計會有超過  10 家新客戶 (Tape-out / New design) 進行導入與設計中。  
預估欣銓 2027 年營收 251.13 億元， YoY+29.60% ，稅前淨利 81.39 億元，
YoY+29.08% ，稅後 EPS 13.20 元：  
展望 2027 年營運前景，欣銓管理層對整體營收增長維持正面樂觀基調，主
要受惠於 2 大核心擴產引擎全面啟動：第一為龍潭新廠無塵室自 2Q27 起進
入逐季開出階段，第二為子公司全智科技新竹湖口新廠產能將有機會達到
全產全銷。  
除晶圓代工廠外包訂單外，美系通訊核心客戶對高階 AI 晶片測試需求自
4Q26 一路強勁延伸至 2027 全年，全智湖口新廠產能已獲客戶全數包下，若
產能利用率達到滿載運作下，預期有機會貢獻 2027 年單月營收 1~1.5 億元。
車用與安控 (ATV/Security) 及儲存裝置 (Storage) 受惠於 Edge AI 與資料中心
需求回溫， 2027 年營收貢獻將持續擴張。  
季度走勢上，延續 4Q26 營收 QoQ+1~3% 的走勢， 1Q27 受到農曆春節工作
天數減少與季節性影響，預估營收將持平於 4Q26 ，毛利率受前期準備費用
影響維持在 40% 以上高檔水準，整體產能利用率 (UTR) 則維持約 80% 健康水
位。真正的成長將自 2Q27 啟動，欣銓龍潭廠共有 7 層無塵室，第 1 層已於
2Q26 年完成認證，自 07/2026 正式開始貢獻營收與量產。第 2 至第 3 層無
塵室工程預計於 2026 年底至 1H27 完工，其中第 2 層產能最快自 2Q27 開始
貢獻營運，依每季啟用一層的節奏逐步推進。  
獲利能力與定價方面，雖然新廠開出初期伴隨折舊與人力整備等成本，但隨
著 AI 測試需求暢旺帶動整體產能利用率持續站穩高檔，加上高階晶圓測試
比重維持高檔，評估 2027 年毛利率將高於 2026 年。此外，公司針對主要長
期配合之大型客戶維持穩定報價，但對於低毛利與零星客戶已啟動調價提
議並陸續反映漲價，進一步強化整體產品組合獲利體質。  
預估欣銓 2027 年營收 251.13 億元， YoY+29.60% ，稅前淨利 81.39 億元，
YoY+29.08% ，稅後 EPS 13.20 元。

10 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

市场研究部 2026年 10月 14日
事件
10月 14日，美国劳工部发布，9月美国 CPI环比上涨 0.6%，
同比上涨 3.7%；季调后，环比上涨 0.6%，同比上涨 3.7%。
解读
一、9 月美国 CPI 环比涨幅扩大，能源价格上涨是主要原因。
9 月美国 CPI 环比上涨 0.6%，比 8 月提高 0.3 个百分点，季
调后，环比上涨 0.6%，比 8月提高 0.2个百分点。
其中，食品价格环比涨 0.1%，与 8 月持平；能源价格环比上
涨 3.7%，比 8 月提高 2.4 个百分点；核心 CPI 环比上涨 0.4%，
比 8月提高 0.1个百分点。
油价环比上涨。9月国际油价（纽约原油价格）均价 92.70 美
元/桶，比 8月均价（78.33美元/桶美元）上涨 18.35%；布伦特原
油价格平均为 97.01 美元/桶，比 8 月均价（82.72 美元/桶美元）
上涨 17.28%。
8大类商品和服务价格环比普涨，与 8月相比，环比涨幅 1降
1平 6升：
其中，交通运输价格环比上涨 0.5%，比 8 月提高 1.1 个百分

点，
服装价格环比上涨 1.7%，比 8月提高 1.2个百分点。
教育与通信价格环比上涨 0.4%，比 8月回落 1.5个百分点，
食品与饮料价格环比上涨 0.1%，与 8月持平，
医疗保健价格环比上涨 0.1%，比 8月提高 0.2个百分点，
其它商品和服务价格环比上涨 0.4%，比 8 月提高 0.3 个百分
点，
住宅价格环比上涨 0.3%，比 8月提高 0.1个百分点，
娱乐价格环比上涨 0.5，比 8月提高 0.5个百分点。
二、9 月美国 CPI 同比涨幅扩大，核心 CPI 同比小幅提高
9 月，美国 CPI 同比上涨 3.7%，比 8 月提高 0.3 个百分点，
自 3月以来，连续 7个月高于 3%。
宏观研究
9月美国 CPI同比涨幅有所扩大

其中，食品价格同比上涨 2.5%，比 8 月回落 0.2 个百分点；
能源价格同比上涨 20.4%，比 8月提高 4.1个百分点；核心 CPI同
比上涨 2.6%，比 8月提高 0.2个百分点。
8 大类商品和服务价格同比普涨，与 8 月同比涨幅相比，4降
4升。其中，
住宅价格同比上涨 3.3%，比 8月提高 0.2个百分点；
服装价格同比上涨 3.4%，比 8月回落 0.2个百分点；
交通运输价格同比上涨 7.9%，比 8月提高 1.7个百分点；
其它商品及服务价格同比上涨 4.1%，比 8 月回落 0.1 个百分
点。
教育与通信价格同比上涨 2.3%，比 8月提高 0.2个百分点；
医疗保健价格同比上涨 1.5%，比 8月回落 0.1个百分点；
娱乐价格同比上涨 2.8%，比 8月提高 0.1个百分点；
食品饮料价格同比上涨 2.4%，比 8月提高 0.2个百分点。
9月，美国住所租金（非季调）环比上涨 0.3%，与 8月相同，
同比上涨 3.2%，比 8月提高 0.2个百分点。

9 月，美国服务（非季调）价格环比上涨 0.3%，比 8 月提高
0.1个百分点，同比上涨 3.2%，比 8月上升 0.1个百分点。
可见，9月除了能源价格同比上涨 20.4%，比 8月提高 4.1个
百分点，波动较大，并带动交通运输价格涨幅波动较大外，其余
消费价格同比波动 0.1~0.2个百分点，比较小。
三、9 月 CPI 数据不支持美联储加息决策。
9月美国 CPI 同比受能源价格上涨影响而涨幅小幅扩大，是在
中东冲突再度发酵诱使油价大涨情况发生的，核心 CPI、居住价格
和服务价格同比均比较平稳。8 月美核心 CPI 同比上涨 2.4%，离
2%的政策目标非常近。9 月 16 日，美联储加息 25 个基点，比较
牵强，即便 9月 CPI同比涨幅小幅扩大，也不支持再次加息。
美联储降息与否不影响中国央行货币政策，央行货币政策空间
仍然较大。
投资建议
1、9 月美国 CPI 同比主要受能源价格影响而小幅扩大，核心
CPI比较平稳。
2、9月美国 CPI数据不支持美联储加息决策。
3、美联储利率政策不会对中国货币政策构成制约，央行货币

政策空间仍然较大。
投 风险提示
1、地缘政治恶化超预期；2、欧美经济衰退超预期。

기아 (000270)

26.3Q Review: 수익성에 대한 입증이 필요  Company Brief

26.3Q Review: 매출액 31.6조원, 영업이익 1.8조원  
기아는 26.3Q 매출액 31.6조원 (+10.1% YoY, -4.4% QoQ), 영업이익 1.8조원 
(+24.3% YoY, -30.8% QoQ, +5.8% OPM) 등의 실적을 기록했다. 26.3Q 영업이익은 
시장 컨센서스 (2.36조원)를 22.9% 하회했다 [iM증권 추정치: 매출액 31.5조원, 
영업이익 2.3조원]. 주요 구성 요소별 내용은 아래와 같다. 
① P: 26.3Q 26.3Q 전동화 차량 판매는 30.0만대 (+47% YoY, 판매 비중 
36.2%)를 기록했는데, 이 중 HEV 17.2 만대 (+46% YoY), EV 12.3 만대 
(+75% YoY)로 구성됨. 미국 HEV 판매가 전년 동기 대비 118% 증가하며 
시장 성장률을 약 4배 상회했고, 특히 서유럽 EV 판매가 전년 대비 163% 
증가함. 단 26.3Q 중 원화가 빠르게 강세로 전환되며 원/달러, 원/유로 
평균 환율은 전분기 대비 각 5.2%, 6.2% 하락함에 따른 – 효과 관찰됨. 
② Q: 26.3Q 도매 판매는 84.7만대 (+7.9% YoY) 를 기록했고, 북미, 유럽, 
인도 등 주요 시장에서 고른 성장 나타남. 26.3Q 소매 판매 또한 82.9만대 
(+6.9% YoY)로 글로벌 산업 수요 (-6% YoY)를 상회. 북미는 HEV, 내수와 
유럽은 EV, 인도는 신형 셀토스와 소넷을 중심으로 판매가 확대됨 (이하 
26.3Q 권역별 도매 판매 YoY, 국내: +2.2%, 북미: +7.6%. 유럽: +19.3%. 
인도: +38.5%). 이란 사태 장기화로 아중동 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 
28.4% 감소했으나, 수요가 견조한 중남미와 CIS로 물량을 전환하며 
감소분을 상당 부분 만회함. 연간 도매 판매 가이던스 (335만대)를 유지함. 
③ C: 26.3Q 관세 비용은 약 0.7조원으로 추정되며, 전년 동기 대비 
5,000억원 감소함 (25.2Q 0.7조원, 25.3Q 1.2조원, 25.4Q 1.0조원, 26.1Q 
0.8조원). 반면 인센티브 증가 및 가격 인하 7,270억원, 서유럽 EV 비중 
확대에 따른 믹스 악화 4,650억원, 원자재 가격 상승 영향 3,680억원 등 
총 1.6조원의 감익 요인이 발생함. 이에 26.3Q 매출원가율은 84.1%로 
전년 동기 대비 3.0%p 상승함 (인센티브 +1.9%p, 재료비 +1.2%p). 다만 
26.3Q 기말 환율 급락하며 판매보증충당부채 환입 규모가 축소되었고 
판매보증비 약 9,000억원 감소. 판매관리비율은 10.1%로 3.7%p 개선됨. 
26.3Q 기아 순이익은 1.29조원으로 전년 동기 대비 9.6% 감소했다. 원화 강세에 
따라 외환환산 관련 이익이 전년 동기 540억원 이익에서 3,718억원으로 손실 
전환됐고, 현대모비스 등 관계사 이익 감소로 지분법손익 또한 360억원 감소했기 
때문이다 (현대차 순이익 감소 논리와 동일). 1) 산업 수요 감소와 중동 판매 차질에도 
권역별 파워트레인 대응을 통해 그룹사 대비 가지고 있는 외형 성장성을 증명했으나, 
2) 유럽 EV 판매 확대 과정에서 발생한 인센티브와 믹스 악화라는 비용 부담, 원자재 
가격 상승이 관세 감소 효과를 대부분 반납했고, 3) 분기 중 급격한 원화 강세가 
더해지며 시장 기대를 크게 하회하는 수익성을 기록했다는 것이 26.3Q 실적 총평이다.

실적 발표 후 주가 급락: 성장에도 불구하고 수익성에 대한 우려  
26.10.30 실적 발표 당일 기아 주가는 11.7% 하락했다. 하루 전 발표된 그룹사 실적 
발표와 상반된 모습이었다. 시장이 이번 실적에서 확인한 우려는 아래와 같다. 첫째, 
성장과 이익의 괴리다. 관세 영향을 제외한 26.3Q 영업이익은 2.52조원으로 전년 
동기(2.70조원) 대비 오히려 1,770억원 감소했다. 판매 증가에 따른 물량 효과 
(+4,420억원)와 환율 효과 (+5,290억원)가 발생했음에도, 인센티브와 믹스 악화로 이를 
대부분 반납한 것이다. 기아의 외형 성장 상당 부분이 유럽 EV 판매 증가에서 나오는데, 
해당 시장에서는 중국 EV 업체와의 경쟁으로 공격적인 프로모션이 불가피하다. 26.3Q 
유럽 권역에서의 중소형 EV 판매가 사업 계획을 크게 웃돌며 EV 믹스는 13.1%에서 
14.8%로 확대됐다. 시장은 이를 M/S를 위해 수익성을 훼손하고 있다고 받아들이는 
것이다. 둘째, 당장 26.4Q에도 유의미한 수익성 개선이 없을 것이라는 점이다. 기아는 
26.3Q 컨퍼런스 콜에서 4Q 영업이익률이 3Q 수준과 크게 다르지 않을 것이며, 연초 
제시한 연간 가이던스 (영업이익 10.2조원, +8.3% OPM) 에 다소 미달할 것이라고 
밝혔다. 중동 분쟁의 영향이 최소 연말까지 이어지고, 원자재와 물류비 부담 또한 
지속될 것이 회사의 판단이다. 무엇보다 원/달러 환율이 26.2Q 평균 약 1,505원에서 
26.3Q 약 1,430원, 현재 1,320~1,340원 수준까지 하락하며 이에 따른 - 영향이 커질 
수 있다. 셋째, 그룹사와의 이익 체력 비교다. 현대차는 26.3Q 파업과 생산 차질로 도매 
판매가 11.3% 감소했음에도 원가 절감을 통해 영업이익이 5.5% 증가했고, 시장 
기대를 상회했다. 반면 기아는 판매가 7.9% 증가했음에도 영업이익이 컨센서스를 20% 
이상 하회했다. 26.3Q 양사 영업이익 격차는 약 8,600억원까지 벌어진 상황이다.  
단기 노이즈를 제외한다면 기아의 기업 가치는 여전히 높다  
그럼에도 당사는 기아의 EV 전략, 낮은 수익성을 이유로 중장기 기업 가치에 대한 
기대를 낮출 이유는 없다고 판단한다. 첫째, 외부 요인을 걷어내면 본원적인 수익 
창출력은 유지되고 있다. 기아는 26.3Q 영업이익률을 낮춘 외부 요인을 원자재 1.2%p, 
중동 판매 차질 0.7%p, 환율 1.7%p 등 총 3.6%p로 분석했다. 즉 중동 분쟁이 
해소되면 상당 부분이 사라질 일회성 요인이다. 둘째, EV 수익성 개선 경로는 HEV가 
걸어온 길과 같다. 기아는 HEV 역시 도입 초기에는 수익을 내지 못했으나 현재는 ICE에 
버금가는 수익성을 내고 있다며, 26~27 년 현재의 EV 전략을 유지하겠다고 밝혔다. 
유럽 ICE 시장은 26.3Q 전년 동기 대비 약 20% 축소된 반면 EV 시장은 50% 이상 
성장하고 있다. 전환기에 스케일을 확보하지 못하면 EV 마진을 개선할 방법도 없다. 
기아는 향후 3년간 배터리, 모터, 히트펌프 등 주요 부품에서 연 10% 수준의 원가 
절감을 추진하고, 2~3년 내 EV를 HEV와 같은 수익성 경로에 올려놓겠다는 계획이다. 
셋째, 중장기 성장 계획을 수정하지 않았다. 기아는 27년 사업 계획이 CID 당시 제시한 
숫자와 큰 차이가 없을 것이며, 중장기 영업이익률 10% 목표도 유지했다. IAA 시행에 
대비해 슬로바키아 공장에서 EV를 전량 생산하고 ICE 국내 수출 방안도 검토 중이다. 
주주환원 측면에서도 TSR 35% 를 지키고, 자사주 매입보다 배당을 우선하겠다는 
입장이다. 10/30 종가와 최소 DPS 기준만으로도 배당수익률은 6.3%에 달하고 있다.

기아 판매관리비 (좌) 판매관리비율 (우)
  26.4Q 기아 매출액 31.8조원, 영업이익 2.2조원 전망 
기아 26.4Q 실적을 매출액 31.8조원 (+13.2% YoY), 영업이익 2.2조원 (+20.8% YoY, 
+7.0% OPM)으로 전망한다. 26.4Q 판매 성장세는 이어지고, 3Q에 집중 반영된 원자재 
부담 일부 완화와 계절적 성수기 효과로 3Q 대비 수익성은 소폭 개선될 것이다. 다만 
1) 원화 강세에 따른 평균 환율 하락, 2) 유럽 EV 인센티브의 높은 수준 유지 (미국은 
할부 금리 상승으로 QoQ 약 200달러 증가, 유럽은 전분기와 유사), 3) 중동 판매 
차질과 원자재, 물류비 부담 지속을 감안하면 의미 있는 반등은 어렵다. 이는 4Q 
영업이익률이 3Q 수준과 크게 다르지 않을 것이라는 회사의 언급과도 궤를 같이한다. 
기아 목표주가 150,000원으로 하향  
기아 목표주가를 26.4Q 추정치 하향을 반영해 150,000원으로 하향한다. 기아의 판매 
경쟁력은 금번 분기에도 증명됐다. 다만 EV 판매 확대가 수익성 개선으로 연결된다는 
점이 숫자로 확인되기 전까지, 시장은 기아의 성장에 할인율을 적용할 것이다. 중동 
분쟁 해소와 EV 원가 절감이 맞물리는 27년이 기아가 수익성을 증명할 시점이 될 
것이며, 6%를 상회하는 배당수익률이 그때까지 주가의 하방을 지지할 것으로 판단된다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.15  
잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기

3Q26 Preview 
기아는 3Q26E 매출액 31.1조원(+8.2% YoY, -6.0% QoQ), 영업이익 2.2조원
(+53.1% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 7.2%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 
물량은 좋았으나, 아쉬운 믹스와 환율 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.8% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.0만대
(+8.6%)를 기록했다. EV·HEV 확대에 따른 ASP 상승은 지속되고 있다. 전쟁으로 인
한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 재료비 부담은 전분기 대비 3,000억~4,000억원 증가할 
것으로 예상된다. EV 판매 비중은 2분기 13%에서 3분기 15%, 유럽은 35%에서 
43%로 확대되며 유럽 내 마진 희석은 불가피하다. 원화 강세에 따른 영업상 부담은 
판매보증충당부채 환입 등으로 상쇄돼 3분기 환율 영향은 제한적일 전망이다. 판매보
증충당부채 환입은 QoQ +5,000억원 수준으로 추정된다. 4분기부터 환율 효과는 부정
적일 것이며, 환율 10원 변동 시 연간 손익 민감도는 약 1,500억원 수준이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 165,000원으로 하향 
미국 조지아 공장의 텔루라이드 HEV(2.8만대)와 HMGMA의 스포티지 HEV(1.0만대) 
신규 생산으로 북미의 견조한 HEV 수요에 대응하며, 2027년 믹스 개선이 기대된다. 
유럽은 EV3·EV5로 점유율을 확대하는 가운데 유럽 내 할인율 하락과 EV 잔존가치 
회복으로 판매 여건이 개선되고 있다. 향후 IAA 규제에 따른 법인·플릿 혜택 제한은 
중국 업체의 원가 우위를 줄일 수 있을 전망이다. 
원화 약세 지속을 반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다.  
다만, 현재 주가는 12M Fwd P/E 기준 4.8배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 
수준, 저가 매수 기회라 판단된다. 현재 배당 수익률은 6% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높은 
배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

목표주가 (하향) 165,000 원 
현재주가 108,600 원 
상승여력 51.9 %

컨센서스 대비

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적 
2026-10-29

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 640,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 285,500 원 
상승여력: 124.2%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 감소 및 환율 하락으로 시장 예상치 하회 
Key: 4Q26~1Q27부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 수주 모멘텀 전망 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 엔진&FDC으로 밸류 매력도 충분. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 시장예상치 하회하는 실적 시현  
3Q26 연결 매출액 5조 7,536억원(+30.2% YoY, -9.1% QoQ), 영업이익 8,992억원
(+61.3% YoY, -13.5% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(9,792억원)를 하회했다. 조업일수 감소
(휴가 10일+중대재해 4일) 및 환율 하락 영향(450억원)과 더불어, 임단협 관련 예상 비
용 중 75%를 이번 분기 선반영 했기 때문이다. 상선 수주 빈티지는 중형 선박 기준 ‘23년 
9.6%, ‘24년 65%, ‘25년 25%, 대형 선박 기준 ‘22년 4%, ‘23년 45%, ‘24년 31%, 
‘25년 20%로, 선가 상승효과 지속되는 중이다. 특히 해양 부문에서 루야 프로젝트의 공정
률 확대 효과 및 C/O로 180억원의 환입 발생으로 전 분기 대비 큰 폭의 이익 성장 시현했
다. 최근 키윗과 미국 내 합작 조선소 설립을 추진하며, 미 군수지원함 초도 2척 수주 및 향
후 물량 건조, 그리고 HII와의 함정 선체블록 하도급 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 64 만원으로 하향 
HD현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 82만원에서 64만원
으로 하향한다. 최근 상선 실적 피크아웃 우려에 따른 조선주 주가 급락으로 멀티플 하향 조
정했다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 
FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지속되고 있는 중이다. 올해 공시 기
준으로 육상발전 엔진 약 1.6GW 수주한 것으로 알려졌으나, 미공시된 약 1.4GW까지 포
함 시 총 3.0GW 이상을 수주한 상황이다. 캐파 증설 기준으로 ‘28 년 1.5GW, ‘29 년 
3GW 캐파에 해당하는 물량을 이미 대부분 채웠으며, 기존 계획 대비 빠른 준공 혹은 공정
률 확대, 더 나아가 추가적인 캐파 증설까지 고려할 수준으로 파악된다. 빠르면 올해 연말 
혹은 내년 초부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 본격 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 
전망이다. 현 주가 레벨에서 상승여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 3Q 실적 및 임단협 불
확성 해소된 현 시점에서 적극적 매수 의견 및 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)로 제시한다.

작성자(한승한)는 본 조사분석자료에 게재된 내용들이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 신의성실하게 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다. 
투자판단 3 단계(6 개월기준) 15%이상 → 매수 / -15%~15% → 중립 / -15%미만 → 매도

### Page 4

Monthly Economics
Sector Analysis
Personal Consumption Expenditures (PCE)
• We've lifted our Q3 expectation for real PCE on incoming data and a slightly firmer consumer
backdrop in the wake of recent revisions.
Consumer spending looks firmer in the third quarter, and we now expect real PCE to rise at a 3.4%
annualized clip. This upward revision reflects recent data, with August consumption coming in firm
with real spending rising by the fastest monthly clip in nearly two years, but also reflects a slightly

firmer consumer backdrop in the wake of the BEA's annual revisions released late September. Although
revisions did not materially alter spending, they lifted the level of household income in recent years,
which also boosted the personal saving rate. The trajectory of real income growth remains weak, but
the consumer still looks to be in a slightly stronger financial position than previously believed, which
helps explain recent resilience in spending and suggests consumers may have a bit more runway ahead.

Investment: Equipment, Intellectual Property Products and Inventories
• The AI build out will lead to another surge in business fixed investment spending in Q3, though the
effect on headline growth will be partly offset by stronger imports.
Business fixed investment spending continues to be supported primarily by the high-tech buildout.
We forecast real equipment investment to rise at around a 16% annualized clip in Q3, but part of
this boost to headline GDP growth will be offset by stronger import growth that is facilitating the

build out. Net imports of computers are tracking to surge in the third quarter, which will provide a
sizable lift to information processing equipment investment, or around half of overall equipment.
And while non-defense capital goods shipments (including aircraft) slipped in August, the near-term
growth trajectory remains supportive of a pickup in equipment. The rebuild in inventories we have
been anticipating is also tracking to be a bit stronger as businesses replenish drawn down product.

Investment: Residential
• The abrupt rise in mortgage rates is likely to dampen residential investment over the next few
quarters. Residential spending should find support next year as longer-term interest rates
moderate somewhat.
We are unlikely to see another positive residential investment reading in Q3. Rising mortgage rates

have sent a chill over builder outlooks, pushing sales expectations to their weakest level in more than
three years. While builders have responded with deeper price cuts, affordability pressures continue
to weigh on single-family sales and construction outlays. Apartment construction is simultaneously
soft amid rising financing costs and modest rent growth, and home improvement spending remains
constrained by high materials costs and interest rates. While residential investment is likely to remain

4 | Economics

### Page 5

Economic Outlook: October 2026 Economics
a drag this year, we expect a slight moderation in longer-term interest rates in 2027, which would
provide a more supportive backdrop.
Investment: Nonresidential Structures
• We have not made material changes to our nonresidential structures forecast. Despite elevated
interest rates, data center and related spending should continue to support structures investment
through our forecast horizon.
Nonresidential structures spending has accelerated sharply, overwhelmingly driven by data centers

and electric power facilities. As data center construction expands geographically and demand rises
for semiconductors, high-tech capex has also started to generate positive spillovers for warehousing
and manufacturing construction. This buildout, which appears relatively insensitive to interest rates,
should be enough to sustain solid growth in structures investment even as spending across most
other categories remains essentially flat. Domestic energy is another green shoot, with elevated prices

supporting ongoing growth in active rotary rigs.

Net Exports & Trade
• An expected drag from trade in Q3 got larger in light of a near-record trade deficit in August, net
exports is now expected to lop off three full percentage points from third quarter growth.

Exports increased in August for the first time in four months, but the increase was more than offset by
an even larger increase in imports. The upshot was a widening in the trade deficit to a near-record gap
of more than $105 billion. Going into the report, we had a 1.9 percentage point drag from net exports
penciled in for Q3 GDP; we revised that to a full 3.0 percentage points drag in light of the record gap.
Even a modest narrowing in the final months of the year would likely mean a lift to headline GDP in

Q4, but the real story here is that trade will remain a headwind throughout the forecast period owing
largely to surging imports of tech-related products amid the AI boom.
Labor Market
• The jobs market remains stable. We continue to expect the unemployment rate to end the year at
4.2% and hold near that level in 2027.
We modestly revised down our near-term payroll growth forecast following the September jobs
report, but otherwise we have left our labor market outlook unchanged. Measures of labor demand

growth continue to point to a stable labor market. Job openings have inched higher on a year-ago
basis but remain broadly steady while initial jobless claims, WARN notices and Challenger job cut
announcements continue to signal low layoffs. Labor force participation appears to be improving after
its sharp pullback in the first half of the year. Looking ahead, we expect payroll growth to average
around 60K per month in 2027, roughly in line with our estimate of breakeven job growth. That should

Economics | 5

### Page 6

Monthly Economics
keep the unemployment rate near 4.2% in 2027 and leave labor market conditions broadly consistent
with "full employment." Notably, our expectation for labor cost growth of ~3.4% is the reason we
continue to reiterate that labor cost growth is not a contributor to the excess inflation seen in the
data.
Inflation
• We expect the core PCE deflator to rise 3.2% and 2.6% Q4/Q4 in 2026 and 2027, respectively.
Headline inflation remains heavily influenced by developments in the Middle East, with higher oil

prices lifting gasoline and diesel prices. Food inflation has been tame this year but looks poised to
strengthen as higher transportation and commodity prices work their way through supply chains.
Beyond food and energy, price pressures remain concentrated in a handful of categories, most notably
airfares and AI-related tech goods. Core goods inflation is showing signs of stabilizing, as waning tariff-
related price pressures have been offset by resilient demand tied to the AI buildout. We expect some

improvement here in 2027. Still-low shelter inflation and cooler price growth for airfares and health
care should help bring about some moderation in core services inflation next year. We expect the core
PCE deflator to rise 3.2% on a Q4-over-Q4 basis in 2026 before slowing to 2.6% in 2027.

Fiscal Policy
• Fiscal year (FY) 2026 ended on September 30, and the federal government tallied a $2.0 trillion
budget deficit (6.5% of GDP). We project the deficit to be unchanged in dollar terms in FY 2027

and a bit smaller as a share of GDP (~6%).
• Another continuing resolution to extend government funding past the December 11 deadline
seems like the most likely outcome at this time for the FY 2027 budget. It is possible Republicans
use the lame duck session of Congress to increase defense spending and raise the debt ceiling.
Looking ahead to the lame duck session of Congress that will follow the midterm elections, Congress
needs to pass a bill that extends government funding past December 11 to avert a government

shutdown. Another continuing resolution that punts the issue into Q1 seems like the most likely
outcome. Perhaps more interesting will be whether Republicans attempt to use budget reconciliation
to pass one more partisan piece of legislation. If they do, we suspect the two things at the top the
agenda will be more funding for national defense and immigration enforcement priorities as well as a
debt ceiling increase. For the latter, raising it in the lame duck would remove some leverage Democrats

could have in 2027 if they recapture one or both chambers. It is still very early, but we estimate the
debt ceiling "X date" by which Congress must act or face default is sometime in late Q3/early Q4 of
next year.
6 | Economics

### Page 7

Economic Outlook: October 2026 Economics
Monetary Policy & Interest Rates
• Our base case forecast is for the FOMC to hike the fed funds rate by 25 bps in December and
then keep the policy rate unchanged in 2027 at 4.00%-4.25%, with risks skewed toward additional
tightening.
As we go to print, the FOMC seems more likely than not to keep the fed funds rate unchanged in
October. But given the rationale under which the Fed started this tightening cycle and the forcefulness

with which Warsh has driven home his displeasure with elevated inflation, it seems entirely plausible
to us that October will be a live meeting. At a minimum, the recent inflation data seem to make a hike
in December a lock. Past December, the outlook will be highly dependent on future inflation data. Our
expectation is that core inflation will cool gradually in 2027, and we still do not see signs of demand-
side inflation in the data. But hiking cycles have an inertial component to them, and the recent inflation

data reset the clock to zero on the Fed seeing several good inflation prints in a row. This creates upside
risk to our base case for the fed funds rate.

International Developments
• Global growth remains resilient, leaving our 2026 and 2027 forecasts unchanged. Our inflation
forecasts are also unchanged, although risks of delayed price pass-through could prolong inflation
pressures into 2027.

• Monetary policy continues to lean toward tightening across advanced economies, while emerging
market central banks are increasingly cautious, either holding rates or easing more gradually as
inflation pressures rebuild.
The balance of risks to global inflation remains tilted to the upside, complicating the monetary policy
outlook even as growth has proven more resilient than expected. Elevated energy prices, particularly
for refined petroleum products, persistent geopolitical uncertainty and El Niño-related food price

risks could prolong inflationary pressures into 2027. While second-round effects still remain relatively
contained, repeated supply shocks are testing central banks’ willingness to look through the temporary
price increases, particularly where inflation expectations remain elevated, even as higher government
bond yields are already contributing to tighter financial conditions.
Across advanced economies, central banks face increasingly difficult trade-offs as persistent inflation

pressures limit the scope for policy accommodation. The Eurozone remains particularly vulnerable
to energy supply disruptions, with elevated natural gas prices and low storage levels adding to
inflation risks. We continue to expect a 25 bps European Central Bank hike in December, followed by a
prolonged hold through 2027. Fiscal concerns are also adding to pressure, particularly in France, where
the selloff in government debt has pushed yields higher. In the U.K., resilient activity and renewed

Economics | 7

### Page 8

Monthly Economics
inflation pressures similarly underpin our expectation for a Bank of England hike in November,
followed by a hold through 2027.
Elsewhere, policy paths are becoming more differentiated. After four hikes this year brought the Cash
Rate to 4.60%, we expect the Reserve Bank of Australia to remain on hold, although renewed inflation
pressures or stronger-than-expected activity could prompt further tightening. We also still expect
the Bank of Canada to hold at 2.25%, with subdued growth weighing against energy-driven inflation

risks, although incoming inflation data over the coming weeks will be critical in determining whether
persistent price pressures warrant tightening monetary policy perhaps as early as the December
meeting. Japan remains an exception to the broader hold outlook: while the yen's recovery provides
some relief from imported inflation, elevated energy prices and import dependence continue to pose
upside risks. We expect the Bank of Japan to hike again by year-end and now expect an additional 25

bps hike in Q3-2027, bringing the terminal rate to 1.75%.
The emerging market story has increasingly shifted from easing toward patience or selective
tightening. In Asia, China's weak domestic demand and persistent property-sector strains continue to
warrant policy support, with AI-related exports providing some offset. We expect a more gradual pace
of Reserve Requirement Ratio cuts alongside targeted monetary and fiscal measures. India faces the

opposite challenge, with elevated food and energy prices and rupee depreciation strengthening the
case for further Reserve Bank of India tightening. In Latin America, peso depreciation and elevated
energy costs pose upside inflation risks for Mexico, although limited exchange-rate pass-through
should allow Banxico to remain on hold while policymakers monitor underlying inflation and inflation
expectations. In Brazil, election-related uncertainty and renewed inflation pressures are likely to delay

further easing, with cuts expected to resume in Q1-2027 once policymakers see more convincing
evidence of disinflation.

### Page 1

SHIFT IN NARRATIVE

After a rough first half of the year, the 
narrative around Indonesia’s economy has 
turned relatively constructive . The macro 
outlook is on the mend, and policy coherence 
has strengthened. Recent budget 
developments have reduced the likelihood of an 
outright fiscal slippage. A more disciplined 
fiscal trajectory should also provide room for 
monetary policy to stay focused on its price 
stability mandate rather than on ad dressing 
financial stability risks due to fiscal concerns.

On the markets front, e ven as external triggers 
impacted the rupiah, the one -sided sharp 
depreciation run in the currency in the first half 
of the year has stalled . Debt markets have  
attracted offshore interest, alongside strong 
demand for shorter-tenor SRBI securities, all of 
which have contributed to the overall capital 
flows picture.

Ahead of MSCI’s final take on the domestic 
equity market’s classification in November, 
however, equity markets  find little favour . 
Nonetheless, material improvement s in the 
stock listing s and regulatory requirements 
undertaken by the stock market and financial 
regulators are expected to contribute to the 
medium-term stability of the capital markets.

Key domestic risks that plagued the economy 
in the first half of the year have been alleviated 
to some extent, while a few other monitorables 
remain.

www.dbs.com/insightsdirect/macro groupresc@dbs.com 
• Indonesia's macro narrative has 
turned more constructive in 
2H26. 
• Key domestic policy concerns 
have eased. 
• Policy alignment has improved. 
• Greater fiscal discipline should 
allow BI to remain focused on 
price stability.  
• The current account is likely to 
widen as the trade surplus 
narrows. 
• Portfolio and FDI inflows should, 
however, narrow the BOP 
shortfall. 
• Few monitorables remain, 
including factors in the 2027 
fiscal math. 
• IDR performance will remain 
heavily influenced by global 
conditions.

NEAR-TERM FISCAL WORRIES 
RECEDE

Fiscal fears have moderated in the near 
term. New Finance Minister Suahasil 
Nazara reaffirmed the government’s 
commitment to maintain the annual 
deficit below -3% of GDP threshold. The 
2026 run-rate suggests that the deficit is 
likely to close within the budgeted target.

By September 2026, the fiscal deficit 
stood at 1.2% of GDP , reflecting a 
normalisation in spending and firm 
revenue growth. Total spending rose by 
a still -strong 1 9% yoy , even though the 
pace moderated from 1H, backed by 
disbursements towards the flagship free 
food program, subsidy and 
compensation outlays , as well as 
personnel expenditure.  Realised 
expenditure stood at 69% of the full -
year budget by 9M26.

On the other hand, revenues rose sharply 
by 25 .5% yoy (partly benefiting from 
base effects as readings were negative 
at the same time last year), helped by 
higher tax collections, export duties, and 
non-tax receipts. Revenues have already 
accounted for 74% of the budgeted 
scale by 9M26, suggesting full -year 
target is likely to be met.

Global crude prices are a key risk for the 
fiscal outlook, as prevailing Brent prices 
are above $100bl at the time of writing 
(vs budgeted ICP at $70bl), which will 
feed through higher subsidy and 
compensation outgoes, as authorities 
remain hesitant to raise fuel pump prices.

Significant efficiency measures have, 
however, been put in place  to contain 
spending, including reductions in 
outlays towards flagship schemes , 
which will help contain the extent of 
deterioration in the fiscal math. For 
instance, the flagship MBG program, 
which started with an allocation of IDR 
35trn, was  moderated to IDR 268trn 
(realised spending by Sep26 was IDR 
153trn). Successive stimulus packages

### Page 3

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            3

have been announced but the scale has 
been contained.

2027 math signals fiscal prudence, soft 
spots warrant attention

The official goalposts for 2027 point to 
a continued preference for fiscal 
prudence rather than fiscal activism.

Spending is on course to surpass the 
symbolic IDR 4000tn level, ris ing nearly 
4% yoy, with outlays divided between 
the centre and regional governments. 
The mix points to steady allocations 
towards subsidies, social transfers , and 
capital expenditure,  with ministry -level 
spending (K/L) set to contract, while non-
K/L spending is budgeted to increase. 
More notably, spending is expected to 
rise on key flagship programs – for 
instance, social protection (up 10%), the 
largest component education (up 14%) , 
which includes the free meals program 
at IDR 240trn.

On the revenue end, state revenues are 
expected to rise by 9% yoy compared to 
the budgeted 2026 APBN/ 7% vs the 
revised 2026 outlook. Compared to 
baseline 2026, tax revenues are 
expected to rise 10.5%, besides higher 
non-tax collections. This math lands the 
2027 budgeted deficit at -2.4% of GDP 
vs revised 2026 at -2.85% of GDP.

Few uncertainties that loom.  Firstly, 
next year’s math rests on an upbeat 
growth projection of 6 .0% (DBSf 5.2%) , 
besides modest depreciation in the 
rupiah and about  a 7% increase in the 
Indonesia crude price assumption (see 
table). Some of these expectations 
might be challenged as the year unfolds. 
Next, state revenues are expected to 
rise to a n upbeat  12.5% of GDP , likely 
riding on a strong growth forecast, 
better tax due diligence , and improved 
administration via the Coretax 
mechanism.

Thirdly, crude prices are a key risk to the 
fiscal math. Given the economy’s 
position as a net oil importer, various 
official commentaries put the sensitivity  
at every $1pb oil worsening the fiscal 
balance by IDR 6trn, after accounting for 
oil-related revenues. This implies that for 
every $10pb increase, the deficit could 
be impacted by ~IDR 60trn, not far from 
MOF’s sensitivity of IDR 68trn (~0.25 -
0.30% of GDP) due to higher subsidy and 
compensation costs  (ICT assumed at 
$75bl vs prevailing Brent at $100bl). 
Spending will need to reprioritised to 
accommodate additional allocation 
towards subsidies and compensation.

### Page 4

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            4

Next, the direction of travel on the 
contribution of SOE dividends from the 
investment agency to the state coffers 
for 2027 remains unclear, after being 
accounted for this year. Lastly, 
successive stimulus measures in-kind 
and wage subsidies were announced this 
year and could marginally add to the 
strain in 2027 (0.1-0.2% of GDP).

Discussions around the State Finance 
Law also warrant a watch. We maintain 
our deficit forecast at a wider -2.8% to -
2.9% range to account for any revenue 
shortfall or a gradual pace of growth. 
Nonetheless, deficits/debt are likely to 
stay below key thresholds  and not 
emerge as a flashpoint for the markets.

MONETARY POLICY ON AN EVEN 
KEEL

Bank Indonesia has left the benchmark 
rate unchanged  since July 2026, after 
100bp hikes in 1H26. While tighter global 
financial conditions and geopolitical 
risks were key risks on the horizon, the BI 
held the domestic GDP forecast at 4.9 -
5.7% (DBSf: 5.3%) alongside 
manageable inflation, notwithstanding 
El Nino-related risks. Inflation rose to 3.3% 
yoy in Sep26, towards the high end of the 
target range. Authorities have, however, 
assured that the impact of higher food 
prices due to poor weather will be 
addressed through administrative 
measures by regional bodies.

Contained inflation expectations ha ve 
reduced the urgency for BI to tighten 
policy further, allowing policymakers to 
prioritise growth and financial stability 
considerations. The emphasis on 
strengthening the balance of payments 
position reflects continued caution over 
external financing conditions and capital 
flows. However, with the rupiah 
expected to remain stable on the back of 
policy measures and fundamenta ls, the 
BI did not view current external 
pressures as severe enough to warrant 
additional tightening.

BI introduced a host of measures at the 
June to September policy meetings , 
including FX monetary hedging 
instruments and a mechanism to provide 
foreign investors with greater flexibility 
in managing IDR-denominated 
investments in Indonesia.  
These included –  
a) swap hedging per tenor bucket was 
increased to 3M - 15%; 6M - 20%; 12M - 
25%, from 12.5% earlier.  
b) DNDF incentive was increased to 6M - 
25% and 12M – 30%. An increase in FX/ 
NDF swap premium incentives for longer 
tenors will lower hedging costs and

### Page 5

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            5

encourage longer tenor positions, in turn 
increasing longevity of these flows;  
c) Launch of the VASTRA scheme (FX 
Market Transactions for Hedging 
through Partner Banks) got underway, 
with the naming of 14 domestic partner 
banks. This facility is designed to help 
investors transact with lower underlying 
documentation requirements, tap into 
longer banking hours , and access 
onshore hedging facilities.  
d) deepen FX and money markets by 
expanding Local Currency Transactions 
(LCT) between IDR and CNY , besides a 
few other currencies.

Policymakers are likely to tap a 
combination of administrative and 
macroprudential measures rather than 
solely interest rates to defend the 
currency as well as keep inflationary 
expectations in check.

We expect the pause on rates to extend 
into 4Q26.  This call might be at risk if 
depreciation pressures on the rupiah 
mount again on unfavourable global 
developments and if the US Fed 
undertakes a more aggressive hik ing 
cycle than our baseline (of 75bp hikes).

WIDER CAD WILL BE PARTLY 
OFFSET BY POSITIVE FLOWS

Indonesia’s 1H26 current account 
deficit (CAD) widened significantly  to -
$16bn vs -$3bn in the comparable period 
a year ago. Much of this deterioration 
was led by a narrower goods trade 
balance, while  the services deficit 
widened on the seasonal travel account 
due to the Hajj period. Higher coupon 
payments weighed on the primary 
account.  This pressure is likely to persist 
in 2H as the oil & gas trade deficit 
remains wide and export performance 
has been fi rm but tracking lower than 
regional peers, likely also impacted by 
the El Nino phenomenon in key agri 
commodities. We expect full year CAD to 
be closer to 1.8-2% of GDP from -0.1% in 
2025.

Much rests on the capital flows account. 
While 1H balance of payments (BOP) also 
widened, there was palpable 
improvement in 2Q26 vs 1Q.  2Q BOP 
narrowed significantly to $0.9bn from a 
wide $9.1bn in 1Q, helped by positive 
capital account flows – primarily 
stronger portfolio investment on SRBI 
and debt inflows, and net FDI position. 
This dependency is likely to extend to 2H, 
with capital fl ows till Sep26 holding up 
on account of hedging discounts and 
moves to improve inflows. Global 
conditions, including the narrow ID -US 
yields, however, warrant attention. 
Nonetheless, full-year BOP could remain

### Page 6

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            6

wide, on a scale comparable to the 2025 
gap.

IN SUMMARY

IDR markets have been relatively 
steady in October despite higher oil 
prices and a bid dollar , though 
sentiment remains sensitive to external 
developments and swings in global risk 
appetite. USDIDR traded within 17600 -
18000, while 10Y yield hovered around 
7.15-7.25%. A jump in the Aug goods 
trade surplus aided this respite, 
alongside foreign purchases of domestic 
debt, which have remained positive this 
month and on a  YTD basis. Insurers, the 
central bank, and retail investors have 
remained key anchors for the bond 
market. SRBI rates are off highs to sub -
7% levels but are attracting steady 
demand, likely encouraged by hedging 
discounts. An improved outlook for flows 
has helped contain spillovers from a 
wider current account deficit. This does 
not take away from the fact that the 
rupiah is amongst the worst performers 
on a year-to-date basis.

Separately, markets will also monitor 
developments in the political economy, 
institutional architecture, and formation, 
as well as the role of state -led agencies 
(e.g., DSI) and progress on the 
centralisation of decision -making. We 
expect the BI to keep on hold, however, 
pressure to tighten policy might return if 
the US Fed hikes rates well into 2027 
and the US dollar benefits from a risk-off 
environment, weighing on the currency.

Industrials: AI Jitters Versus Booming Orders

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Industrials: AI Jitters Versus
Booming Orders
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 28 Oct 2026
View in browser
Photo by Ant Rozetsky / Unsplash

I. AI Still Boosting Capital Goods Orders
The AI investment boom has been a double-edged sword for S&P 500
Industrials this year. Back in July, we noted that the sector was the
second-best performer among the S&P 500's 11 sectors, with a 17.7% ytd
gain, as investors favored companies supplying the equipment and
infrastructure needed for the AI buildout. Since then, however, intensifying

### Page 2

AI jitters have weighed on industrial stocks, fueled by a potentially toxic
trifecta of financial constraints: elevated borrowing costs, uncertain
investment returns, and concerns about whether sufficient capital will be
available to finance everyone's ambitious AI spending plans.
The corresponding reversal has been significant. As of October 27,
Industrials was up just 8.2% ytd, compared with a 12.8% gain for the S&P
500, slipping to sixth place among the 11 sectors (chart).

The sector's stock price index is trading below its 200-day moving average
but remains on a solid multi-year uptrend (chart).
Performance within the sector has been uneven so far this year, with rail
transportation leading, while industrial machinery and aerospace and
defense lagging (chart).

### Page 3

We think the recent selloff is overdone and continue to recommend
overweighting Industrials. For starters, the sector's earnings outlook
remains upbeat. Forward earnings rose 13.6% ytd to a record high last
week (chart).
The sector's forward profit margin is also at a record high of 11.7% (chart).

### Page 4

Analysts' net revisions of their revenues and earnings estimates have also
improved significantly in recent months, with both remaining historically
elevated (chart).
Furthermore, analysts still expect double-digit earnings growth of 17.6% in
2026 and 16.5% in 2027 (chart).

### Page 5

The sector's forward P/E has fallen sharply recently, but remains above
the overall market's valuation multiple (chart).
The AI investment boom continues despite the stock market's jitters about
the AI trade. The five major hyperscalers (Amazon, Alphabet, Microsoft,
Meta, and Oracle) are projected to spend nearly $800 billion on capex this
year and more than $1 trillion in 2027. So far this week, there has been
roughly $235 billion in announced AI financing, including up to $95 billion

from Meta, a $40 billion chip-and-server leasing arrangement involving
SpaceX, and a potential $100 billion IPO by Anthropic. We therefore
continue to recommend overweighting Industrials, which should continue
to benefit from the buildout.

### Page 6

Indeed, the AI spending boom is still boosting industrial activity, with much
more in the pipeline. Consider the following:
(1) Durable goods. September durable goods orders rose 1.8% m/m, the
largest increase in five months. Orders excluding transportation increased
0.5%, while core capital goods orders (nondefense excluding aircraft), a
key gauge of business investment, rose 0.5% and 13.5% y/y to another
record high (charts).
The major components of durable goods orders are at record highs

(chart).

### Page 7

Orders for machinery necessary to operate data centers are especially
strong (chart).
Unfilled orders for computers and electronic products rose to a record
$162.4 billion in June (chart).

### Page 8

(2) Regional business surveys. All five regional Fed manufacturing
surveys are now available for October. The regional manufacturing PMI
edged down to 13.8 from September but remained comfortably in
expansion territory, suggesting that the national ISM M-PMI likely
remained above 50.0 in October (chart).
The average prices-paid index across regional Fed surveys stood at 57.0,
near levels last seen in 2022. Meanwhile, the prices-received index
climbed to 31.6, suggesting that manufacturers are increasingly passing

higher input costs on to customers (chart).

### Page 9

II. US Economy
The industrial sector isn't the only driver of the US economy showing
remarkable strength. Another key driver of economic resilience is
consumer spending. Here is more:
(1) Consumer spending. Redbook same-store sales rose 9.7% y/y
during the week of October 16. That is well above the 2025 average of
5.8% (chart).
(2) Labor market. Consumers' perceptions of labor-market conditions
improved in October. The percentage saying jobs were plentiful rose to

26.7%, the highest since April (chart).

### Page 10

The latest weekly ADP data show private-sector employment rising by an
average of 16,750 jobs per week over the four weeks through October 10,
or roughly 67,000 jobs on a monthly basis (chart). The labor market
remains in good shape.
(3) GDP. Ahead of Thursday's release of the Q3-2026 US GDP report, the
Atlanta Fed's final GDPNow estimate suggests that the economy
expanded at a robust 3.4% annualized rate during the quarter, supported
by strong consumer spending and business investment (chart).

clause.

WEEKLY AGGREGATES
ITEM DEFINITIONS AND METHODOLOGY
3© 2026 LSEG.  All marks herein are used under licence. TRPR_82222_875
General:
All aggregates are bottom-up aggregates calculated from analysts' consensus forecasts unless otherwise noted. Bottom-up
aggregates are weighted calculations using the I/B/E/S data from LSEG share class monitored or the official index weight of
the index-provider. Top-down forecasts are compilations of strategists' estimates. I/B/E/S universe calculations are based on

all companies within a given market monitored by LSEG.
Benchmark index calculations, including those for official local market indices, the S&P 500 Index, and the FTSE World Equity
Indices are calculated using official index constituents and weightings. All index composites are representative of current
index constituents. Index constituents and weightings are updated monthly during options expiration (the Thursday before the
third Friday of every month) for every index except the S&P 500, the constituents of which are current as of the date of

calculation.
PEIGBY refers to the Price Earnings Inverse Government Bond Yield ratio, which measures equity valuation with respect to
the inverse-yield on long-term government bonds (now known as the 'Fed' model). All PEIGBY ratios are I/B/E/S universe-
specific except for the US ratio, where it is based on the S&P 500 Index.
Methodology:
For US and selected Canadian aggregates, calendar-year aggregation is performed using the sum of four quarters' estimates

and a quarterly pro-ration is applied to non-December year end companies' quarterly EPS estimates. Outside of the US and
Canada, all company-level data are calendarized prior to aggregation using the Compustat method of calendarization.
Aggregation is performed using the annual consensus EPS estimate and a May roll-back is applied to December year-end
markets. Aggregates for Japan, where most companies report in March, use an August rollback and a November rollback is

used for Australia and New Zealand, where the fiscal year-end for most companies is in June.
All 12-month-forward data for ex-US markets are calculated using a pro-rata weighting to annual EPS forecasts. The
weightings applied are a daily calibration out of 365 days relative to each constituent's individual fiscal year-end and the
current date.
All non-surprise data, including aggregate forecast earnings, forecast growth, and forward-looking valuation calculations are

weighted according to the I/B/E/S share class monitored for I/B/E/S universes or the weightings assigned to each constituent
in a given index. Aggregated earnings calculations are the equivalent of net profit aggregates, as they are derived from the
EPS forecasts aggregated to the net income level by the appropriate share weighting.
Earnings Aggregation:
For 12-month-forward earnings, the 12-month-forward EPS is used in place of the calendarized EPS in the formulas below.

I/B/E/S universe EPS Aggregate = Index EPS Aggregate =
Growth Calculations:
All growth calculations are the weighted year over year growth in earnings for the calendarized fiscal year period. The
formulas below use index shares for index growth calculations.
Calendarized Earnings Growth = 12-month-forward Growth =
Valuation:
All valuation calculations are the weighted-average price-earnings ratio for the fiscal period in question. The formulas below

use index shares for index P/E calculations.
Calendarized P/E Aggregate = 12-month-forward P/E =
Revisions Momentum:
Unless otherwise indicated, analysts' revisions momentum is a simple aggregation of the total number of estimates raised
divided by the total number of estimates lowered. Thus, any revisions ratio less than 1 indicates negative revisions
momentum while a ratio greater than 1 indicates positive revisions momentum, with 1 marking parity.

The Global Aggregates from LSEG product contains annual aggregates for universes included in this publication, including
supplemental aggregate data of over 100 items as well as history dating back to 1985 for North America and to 1987 for other
markets. To learn more about this product, contact your LSEG sales representative.
FTSE and the FTSE World Equity Indices are owned by FTSE International Limited.

Editorial 
Opportunities from the bond sell-off  
Strategy summary: The global bond sell-off is creating tactical 
and medium-term opportunities. In the US, September’s yield 
surge has improved the risk -reward in 10 -year government 
bonds, presenting an attractive entry point for investors with a 
6-12-month horizon. We believe bond and money markets are 
overly hawkish about the Fed outlook, given disinflation is likely 
to return as impact of oil prices and tariffs ease next year. 
In equities, we stick to quality, especially sectors riding the AI -
led earnings boom. The upcoming Q3 earnings season is likely 
to show AI -investments are powering sectors beyond the 
technology and communications services, such as financials, 
materials and power and electrification. We remain constructive 
on the semiconductor and memory segments. We also expect 
the US Dollar to peak in the next few weeks along with short -
to-medium-term US bond yields. This creates opportunities for 
AUD corporate bonds, Emerging Market USD bonds and gold. 
Attractive risk -reward in US 10 -year government bonds.  
The US 10-year bond yield rose more than 50bps in September 
(biggest monthly rise since 2022) and 115bps this year, amid 
expectations of economic resilience, higher Fed rates, fuel -
driven inflation, AI-related capital demand and fiscal concerns. 
Valuations appear close to fair value, improving the 6-12-month 
risk-reward. Bond arithmetic suggests losses of less than 1% if 
the yield rises to 6%, vs. more than 10% gains if it falls to 4.5%. 
Resilient US economic activity, but softer -than-expected 
inflation. September data showed robust US employment 
(jobless claims, ADP) and core capital-goods orders. However, 
consumer confidence remains depressed, real disposable 
income is flat and households are drawing on savings to sustain 
spending. Against this divided backdrop, core PCE inflation - 
the Fed’s preferred gauge - was softer than expected at 0.2% 
m/m and 3.0% y/y. A revised method to measure PCE inflation 
also indicate that annual core PCE inflation was 35bps lower 
than previously reported, narrowing the gap with CPI inflation. 
Markets appear overly hawkish on the Fed. Money markets 
price about 85bps of tightening over the next 12 months. A 
strong labour market (September’s non -farm payrolls data is 
due tonight) may justify one or two further hikes, but aggressive 
tightening after the sharp rise in bond yields – which has 
already tightened financial conditions - could threaten financial 
stability. US small- and mid-cap equities have already fallen 8–
9% from recent peaks over the past couple of months, 
materially underperforming large caps, as energy and 
borrowing-cost concerns intensify. W hile some Fed speakers 
remain hawkish, we  agree with New York Fed President 
Williams’ that there is no urgency to tighten. We see little 
chance of an October Fed rate hike and expect inflation data 
for Sep-Nov determining whether the Fed moves in December. 
Stay with quality equities and AI beneficiaries.  US large 
caps have absorbed the rise in bond yields relatively well, 
supported by expectations of robust earnings growth led by AI-
related investment. We continue to favour quality companies 
and sectors benefiting from this AI cycle. Semiconductors and 
memory remain preferred exposures: structural AI demand 
should extend the memory upcycle, while long -term supply 
agreements provide better revenue visibility. The opportunity is 
also broadening beyond technology and communication 
services into financials, materials, power and electrification. 
Opportunities in AUD corporate bonds and gold.  The USD 
rebound alongside higher US yields offers a tactical opportunity 
to add higher -yielding Emerging Market USD and non -USD 
bonds. We remain positive on AUD corporate bonds, which 
offer a yield premium over comparable US credit. Our 
constructive medium-term AUD/USD view could add returns for 
USD-based investors over the next 6 –12 months. We would 
also use gold’s recent pullback to add exposure, supported by 
sustained Emerging Market central bank demand. We continue 
to view USD 4,000/oz as important technical support for gold.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

Top client questions 
  Do you expect more RBA rate hikes after its less hawkish 
guidance? What is your view on the AUD and AUD bonds? 
Our view: We expect the RBA to leave rates unchanged, but 
retain the option to hike again should inflation intensify. We 
maintain our medium-term constructive AUD view and maintain 
our opportunistically bullish AUD corporate bonds idea. 
Rationale: The RBA’s forward guidance remains data dependent, 
explicitly allowing further tightening if needed as inflation remains too 
high.  At 4.60%, Australia’s cash rate is currently 60–85bp above the 
Fed’s 3.75–4.00% range. Thus, even with our two additional 25bp s 
Fed hikes expectation, Australian rates would hold a modest positive 
rate differential. We expect the AUD to strengthen once the Fed 
signals that US rates are near their peak, which should allow resilient 
Australian fundamentals to reassert themselves. Key risks are 
persistently high US yields, weaker China growth or a sharper 
deterioration in global risk sentiment. 
We believe risk-reward has become more attractive for AUD bonds 
following the bear -flattening of the AUD yield curve. Markets are 
already pricing in one more RBA hike through end-2027, presenting 
an attractive opportunity to earn a yield even as increasingly 
restrictive financial conditions reinforc e disinflation over time. 
Meanwhile, the positive interest rate differential with the US supports 
the currency outlook, supporting the case for AUD bonds. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  What does the incoming US data tell us about the path for 
US 10-year bond yields from here? 
Our view: The risk -reward in US 10 -year yields has turned 
attractive following their move higher amid incoming US data. 
Rationale: Recent US data has indeed started to show softer-than-
expected inflation. However, market technicals are likely temporarily 
delaying a move lower in bond yields, with reports suggesting the  
rise in yields is creating forced selling amongst hedge funds and real 
estate investment trusts. Nevertheless, we do not expect this to be 
an extended selloff. We have initiated an opportunistic bullish idea 
on US 10-year government bonds this week and continue to believe 
this idea offers an attractive risk/reward on a 6-12-month horizon as 
yields become attractive enough to entice buyer flow to return. On a 
broader allocation basis, we retain our structural preference for 3-7-
year maturities in bonds. 
— Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

Australia-US interest rate differentials and positive 
outlook for the AUD

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How should investors position in the GBP ahead of the UK 
budget announcement next month? 
Our view: We maintain a medium-term constructive GBP view, 
but keep near-term exposure measured ahead of the 28 October 
Budget. We would view any pre -Budget weakness as an 
opportunity to gradually build GBP exposure. 
Rationale: The government’s decision to adjust the pension triple 
lock from 2030 is an early indication of greater willingness to tackle 
longer-term spending pressures, reinforcing Chancellor Healey’s 
commitment to fiscal discipline and meeting the fiscal rules. Markets 
are likely to scrutinise the Budget closely for evidence that this 
discipline extends to near -term tax and spending decisions. A 
credible Budget should help contain pressure on UK government 
bond yields and support GBP. Any fiscal slippage remains a key risk.  
Near term, we prefer measured GBP exposure as elevated US yields 
and repricing towards further Fed tightening could continue to 
support the USD. Over the medium term, we expect GBP to rebound 
as the Fed eventually signals the peak of its tightening cycle. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  How would different US mid-term election outcome 
scenarios affect the outlook for US equities? 
Our view: We believe election outcomes will have a limited 
impact over the next 12 months. We remain positive on US 
equities driven by fundamental earnings growth.  
Rationale: Over the past 50 years, the ruling US President’s party 
has retained control of both chambers of Congress on only two 
occasions (1978 and 2002). All other elections result ed in an 
opposition sweep or a divided government.  
Based on the latest prediction markets, it appears that an opposition 
sweep (both the House and Senate flips from Republican to 
Democrats) is more likely than a divided government ( in which the 
House flips to Democrats , while the Senate remains under 
Republican control). A scenario where Republicans retain a majority 
is least likely per prediction markets. 
While mid -term elections may introduce near -term volatility, we 
caution against overreaction. Historically, the average return for the 
S&P 500 following an opposition sweep is 17% over the next 12 
months, but the average return following a divided government is not 
significantly worse, at 15%. We prefer to base our investment views 
on fundamental earnings growth, where investor attention should 
focus on the upcoming Q3 earnings season. 
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst

opportunity to gradually build GBP exposure over
